# Agents · U · part 5

910 entries, UX Architect to ux-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **UX Architect**: Technical architecture and UX specialist who provides developers with solid foundations, CSS systems, and clear implementation guidance ([criptogus/agent-evolve-network](https://github.com/criptogus/agent-evolve-network/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-ux-architect.md) · ★235 · 52 copies)
- **UX Architect**: Use when designing or reviewing UX for the target UI repo: information architecture, navigation, user flows, interaction patterns, input/keyboard model, list/detail/now-content behavior, search scope, first-run/onboarding, and empty/loading/error states. Platform-agnostic: grounds in the repo's Storybook + ui-map and the platform knowledge pack. Use for 'how it works', not visual styling. ([dragoshont/architrave](https://github.com/dragoshont/architrave/blob/HEAD/agents/ux-architect.agent.md) · ★2)
- **UX Architect**: Specjalista ds. architektury technicznej i UX, który zapewnia deweloperom solidne fundamenty, systemy CSS i jasne wskazówki implementacyjne ([neurise-io/neurise-adseo](https://github.com/neurise-io/neurise-adseo/blob/HEAD/agents/design-ux-architect.md) · ★2)

TRIGGER when: "UX 설계해줘", "플로우 설계", "레이아웃 설계", "와이어프레임", "디자인 검토해줘", "UI 비평", "UI 개선해줘", "사용성 점검", "디자인 시스템 일관성 검토", 새 기능/페이지 구현 전 UX 설계 필요, 인터랙션 패턴 정의, 반응형 전략 수립 DO NOT TRIGGER when: UI 구현·시안 생성(ui-publisher 사용), 단순 스타일 변경, 기존 컴포넌트 수정, 기획서 작성(product-planner 사용), 컴포넌트 구현·Storybook story 작성(메인 세션이 처리) ([jsg3121/poke-korea](https://github.com/jsg3121/poke-korea/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ux-designer.md))
- **ux-designer**: Produces a concise, accessible UX brief (ux.md) with user flows, all interaction states, and accessibility annotations. Hands heavy visual/build work to the impeccable or ui-ux-pro-max skills rather than reinventing it. ([julianhernandez2155/claude-code-harness](https://github.com/julianhernandez2155/claude-code-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/ux-designer.md))
- **ux-designer**: UX and interface designer for web and CLI products. Turns a product brief into user journeys, screen states, copy and UI acceptance checks that a browser test can run.
